Conventional text-to-speech (TTS) augmentation struggles to cover diverse dialects as it requires a certain amount of real dialect speech. We propose synthesizing pseudo-dialect speech by converting LLM-generated dialect text via a standard-language TTS model, requiring zero real dialect speech. Additionally, we introduce intermediate standard-text prediction during training, acting as semantic normalization","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"glance","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-08","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2610_09321.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","eess.AS"],"summary":{"core_problem":"语音语言模型在方言上因数据稀缺而显著退化，且传统 TTS 增广需要真实方言语音作为种子。","importance":"属于“合成数据 + 训练目标”缓解分布外口音/方言的工作；对音频/语音 LM 的数据构造有参考，但更偏下游（语音到英文翻译）验证。","technical_approach":"用 LLM 生成方言文本，再用标准语 TTS 合成“伪方言语音”，实现零真实方言语音的增广；训练中加入一个中间任务：先预测标准语文本（语义归一化），再服务下游任务以缩小语义鸿沟。","experiments":"任务为方言语音到英文的语音翻译，覆盖日语/德语/中文方言；相对“合成标准语音”基线，伪方言增广带来小幅提升（如日语 25.38→26.24，德语 31.57→32.47）；加入中间标准文本预测后提升更明显（如日语到 28.26，中文 11.67→16.37）。未见预训练规模与更广泛 ASR/SLM 基准。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Speech LMs degrade on dialects due to data scarcity, while conventional TTS augmentation needs real dialect speech.","importance":"A synthetic-data plus auxiliary-objective approach for dialect robustness; useful as a data construction recipe for speech/audio LMs, though validated mainly on a downstream speech-translation setup.","technical_approach":"Generate dialect text with an LLM, synthesize pseudo-dialect speech using a standard-language TTS (zero real dialect speech). Add an intermediate objective to predict standard text as semantic normalization to bridge dialect-semantic gaps.","experiments":"Dialect-to-English speech translation on Japanese/German/Chinese dialects. Pseudo-dialect augmentation improves over synthetic standard-speech baselines (e.g., JA 25.38→26.24, DE 31.57→32.47). Intermediate standard-text prediction yields larger gains (e.g., JA to 28.26, ZH 11.67→16.37). thus small-batch optimizer choices do not extrapolate to large-batch."},"why_scales":{"zh":"这是训练动力学的结构性变化，不是某个数据集或模型尺寸的偶然：batch 直接控制梯度噪声尺度，而噪声进入预条件化（如 Adam 二阶矩、动量滤波、Muon 类预处理）后会改变有效步长与各方向更新的相对权重。随着 batch 增大，噪声主导→曲率主导的过渡会让“同一 optimizer 在不同 batch 下的最优区域”发生形变，出现方向依赖的 scaling rule（从 sqrt 到 linear 的切换）。这种机制在更大模型/更长训练中更稳定，因为大规模训练更依赖大 batch 吞吐，且曲率各向异性与预条件化效应更显著，秩反转不容易被算力放大抹平。","en":"This is a structural shift in training dynamics, not a dataset/model-size accident: batch size directly sets gradient-noise scale, and once noise is fed through preconditioning (Adam second-moment, momentum filtering, Muon-like transforms), it changes effective step sizes and per-direction update weighting. As batch grows, the regime transition from noise-dominated to curvature-dominated deforms each optimizer’s optimal region, yielding direction-dependent scaling rules (sqrt-to-linear switch). This tends to be more stable at larger scale because large training runs rely on large batches for throughput, and curvature anisotropy plus preconditioning effects become more pronounced—so rank reversal is not washed out by 100× compute."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证“batch 改变最优优化器排序”是否在 150M–1.5B 的 proxy ladder 上稳定出现，并提取可用于大规模训练的选择准则（按 batch 分段选 optimizer/超参，而非单一 recipe）。","en":"Validate whether batch-driven optimizer rank reversal reliably appears on a 150M–1.5B proxy ladder, and extract a large-scale actionable rule (batch-segmented optimizer/hparam choice rather than a single recipe)."},"data_sources":{"zh":"同一文本混合（固定 tokenizer、固定去重策略），训练 token 数按 Chinchilla-ish 比例随模型放大；模型规模 150M / 350M / 1.3B（或 1.5B）。对每个规模做 iso-FLOPs：通过调整训练步数使总 token×参数近似匹配。batch 轴：global batch = {256, 2k, 16k}（按硬件可行缩放），并保持序列长度不变。","en":"Same text mixture (fixed tokenizer and dedup), token budget scaled Chinchilla-ish with model size; model ladder 150M / 350M / 1.3B (or 1.5B). For each size, enforce iso-FLOPs by adjusting steps so total tokens×params roughly match. Batch axis: global batch = {256, 2k, 16k} (hardware-feasible), with fixed sequence length."},"steps":[{"zh":"步骤 1：在每个 batch 上分别做“充分调参”的 optimizer shootout（AdamW、Adafactor/AdamW变体、Muon），每个 optimizer 至少扫 {lr, beta/eps, wd} 的小网格；记录最优验证 loss 与训练稳定性（spike/NaN 率）。对照组：沿用小 batch 最优超参直接迁移到大 batch。","en":"Step 1: For each batch, run a thoroughly tuned optimizer shootout (AdamW, Adafactor/AdamW variants, Muon), sweeping at least {lr, beta/eps, wd}; record best validation loss and stability (spike/NaN rate). Control: directly transfer the small-batch best hyperparams to large batch."},{"zh":"步骤 2：做跨尺度验证与秩反转检测：在 150M 上得到的“batch-分段最优 optimizer/超参”迁移到 350M/1.3B，仅允许 µP/学习率按常规规则缩放；比较其在各 batch 下的排名是否保持。额外消融：固定 optimizer，仅改变 eps（或二阶矩平滑）以验证“噪声-预条件化”机制。","en":"Step 2: Cross-scale validation and rank-reversal detection: transfer the 150M-derived batch-segmented best optimizer/hparams to 350M/1.3B, allowing only standard µP/LR scaling; test whether rankings persist per batch. Extra ablation: fix optimizer and vary eps (or second-moment smoothing) to validate the noise–preconditioning mechanism."}],"eval_signal":{"zh":"主信号：分布内验证集 NLL（按 token 加权）在固定训练 FLOPs 下的最优值；辅信号：训练稳定性（loss spike 频率、梯度范数尾部）、以及“batch 从 256→16k 时 optimizer 排名的 Kendall τ”。","en":"Primary: in-distribution validation NLL (token-weighted) at fixed training FLOPs; secondary: stability (loss-spike frequency, gradient-norm tail), and Kendall τ of optimizer ranking when batch increases 256→16k."},"success_criteria":{"zh":"成功：在 ≥2 个模型尺度上观察到一致的秩反转（Kendall τ ≤ 0.2 或出现 top-1 optimizer 互换），且“按 batch 重新调参”相对“直接迁移小 batch 最优”在大 batch 上带来 ≥0.05–0.10 NLL 改善或等价的困惑度下降，并显著降低 spike/NaN。证伪：排名在各 batch 下高度一致（τ ≥ 0.8）且迁移不劣于重调参。","en":"Success: consistent rank reversal on ≥2 model sizes (Kendall τ ≤ 0.2 or top-1 swap), and batch-specific retuning beats direct small-batch transfer on large batch by ≥0.05–0.10 NLL (or equivalent ppl drop) while reducing spikes/NaNs. Requires strict iso-FLOPs, unified numerical-stability settings, and comparable search budgets per batch."},"source_paper_ids":["2610.08975"],"scaling_confidence":0.82},{"pillar":"post_trainability","title":{"zh":"仅调输出层epsilon抑制遗忘","en":"Output-layer epsilon to suppress forgetting"},"claim":{"zh":"在 continual pretrain / domain-adapt 中，灾难性遗忘主要局部化在“新语料几乎不出现 token”的输出 embedding/LM head 行；其机制是这些 token 持续收到单边 softmax 偏置梯度，Adam 的二阶矩归一化会把小而持续的偏置放大成近满幅更新；因此只对输出层提高 Adam epsilon（或等价地降低二阶矩敏感度）即可显著缓解遗忘且较少伤害新学习。","en":"In continual pretrain/domain adaptation, catastrophic forgetting localizes to output embeddings/LM-head rows for tokens rarely seen in new data; mechanism: these tokens receive persistent one-sided softmax bias gradients, and Adam’s second-moment normalization amplifies small-but-consistent bias into near-full updates; thus increasing Adam epsilon only for the output layer (or equivalently reducing second-moment sensitivity) significantly reduces forgetting with minimal harm to new learning."},"why_scales":{"zh":"该现象由 softmax 输出层的梯度几何与 Adam 归一化共同决定，和模型宽度/深度无关：只要词表里存在“在新域缺失但在旧域重要”的 token，输出层就会持续收到方向一致的负梯度（把 logit 往下推），而 Adam 在低方差梯度下会给出过大的有效步长。更大模型通常词表更大、跨域缺失 token 更多，且继续预训练更常见，因此风险不随规模消失，反而更可能累积。干预只改一层的数值尺度，不依赖特定任务或数据配方，属于稳定的训练动力学修正，秩反转概率低。","en":"This is driven by softmax output-layer gradient geometry plus Adam normalization, largely independent of depth/width: whenever the vocab contains tokens absent in new-domain data but important in the old domain, the output layer receives persistent one-direction gradients (pushing logits down), and Adam can yield overly large effective steps under low-variance gradients. Larger models often have larger vocabs and more cross-domain missing tokens, and continual pretraining is more common—so the risk does not vanish with scale and may accumulate. The intervention changes numeric scaling in one layer, not task-specific recipes, making it a stable dynamics fix with low rank-reversal risk."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"在不使用旧数据回放的前提下，验证“遗忘主要在输出层缺失 token”与“仅调 LM head 的 Adam epsilon 即可修复”的因果性，并量化对新域学习的副作用边界。","en":"Without old-data replay, validate the causal link between forgetting and missing-token output embeddings, and test whether changing Adam epsilon only for the LM head fixes it while quantifying side effects on new-domain learning."},"data_sources":{"zh":"Proxy continual pretrain：Base 先在通用语料训练到同等 loss；随后做 domain-adapt（如 code-only 或 math-heavy）1–5B tokens（按模型大小缩放）。模型：350M 与 1.3B 两档。关键是让新域显著改变 token 频次分布（大量 token 近零计数）。","en":"Proxy continual pretrain: train a base on general corpus to matched loss; then domain-adapt (e.g., code-only or math-heavy) for 1–5B tokens (scaled by model size). Models: 350M and 1.3B. Key is a new domain that sharply shifts token frequency (many tokens near-zero count)."},"steps":[{"zh":"步骤 1：对照组用标准 AdamW（统一 lr/wd/warmup）。干预组仅对输出投影/LM head 参数使用更大的 epsilon（例如 1e-8→1e-4/1e-3，做 2–3 档），其余层保持不变；可选再加一组“仅冻结 LM head”用于定位遗忘子空间。","en":"Step 1: Control uses standard AdamW (same lr/wd/warmup). Intervention changes epsilon only for output projection/LM head (e.g., 1e-8→1e-4/1e-3, 2–3 levels), keeping all other layers identical; optionally add 'freeze LM head only' to localize the forgetting subspace."},{"zh":"步骤 2：做 token-frequency 分桶评测：按新域 token 计数把词表分成 {0, 1–10, 10–1k, >1k} 桶，分别测旧域 held-out 的 per-token NLL 变化与 logit shift；同时测新域 held-out NLL 以确认不牺牲适配。跨尺度复现 350M→1.3B，检查效应是否保持。","en":"Step 2: Token-frequency bucket evaluation: bucket vocab by new-domain counts {0, 1–10, 10–1k, >1k}, measure old-domain held-out per-token NLL change and logit shift; also measure new-domain held-out NLL to ensure adaptation is not sacrificed. Replicate across 350M→1.3B to test scale robustness."}],"eval_signal":{"zh":"核心信号：旧域 held-out 上“缺失 token 桶”的 ΔNLL（或 Δlogit mean）与整体困惑度回退；辅信号：输出 embedding 行向量范数/方向漂移（cosine drift）按 token 桶统计；新域 held-out NLL 作为副作用约束。","en":"Primary: ΔNLL (or mean Δlogit) on the old-domain held-out for the 'missing-token' buckets and overall perplexity regression; secondary: output-embedding row norm/directional drift (cosine drift) by token bucket; new-domain held-out NLL as a side-effect constraint."},"success_criteria":{"zh":"成功：相对对照组，epsilon-head 干预在“新域计数=0”桶上将旧域 ΔNLL 回退幅度降低 ≥30%（或将该桶的 logit 下移量降低 ≥30%），同时新域 held-out NLL 劣化不超过 1%（或在统计噪声内）。证伪：遗忘不集中在缺失 token 桶，或 epsilon-head 无法显著改善而冻结/回放才有效。","en":"Success: vs control, epsilon-head reduces old-domain ΔNLL regression for the 'count=0' bucket by ≥30% (or reduces logit downward shift by ≥30%), while new-domain held-out NLL degrades by ≤1% (or within noise). Falsified if forgetting is not concentrated in missing-token buckets, or epsilon-head yields no significant improvement while freezing/replay is required."},"compute_tier":"small"},"risk":{"zh":"边界风险：若继续预训练使用了强正则/权重衰减、或采用非 Adam 类优化器（如 SGD/Adafactor 变体），遗忘机制可能迁移；另外 tokenizer/词表设计（BPE vs unigram、特殊 token）会改变“缺失 token”的定义与梯度形态。","en":"Boundary risks: strong regularization/weight decay during continual pretrain, or non-Adam optimizers (SGD/Adafactor variants) may change the forgetting mechanism; tokenizer/vocab design (BPE vs unigram, special tokens) alters what 'missing tokens' mean and the gradient shape."},"source_paper_ids":["2610.09835"],"scaling_confidence":0.79}]},"dates":"$9:0:props:dates","currentDate":"2026-10-09"}]}]}]
